| AC-EZD-235-3 | EZD-235b | done | `EZD-235b S1 serveJobs answers each request once with its output or an error code`; `EZD-235b S2 serveJobs ignores malformed requests and replies once when the output cannot be posted`; `EZD-235b P1 the pool starts workers on first run, caps them and queues first in first out`; `EZD-235b P2 replies resolve or reject the running job and stray replies are ignored`; `EZD-235b P3 a crashed worker fails its job, is ended and reported once, and the next job gets a new worker`; `EZD-235b P4 dispose ends every worker and fails running, queued and later jobs`; `EZD-235b P5 pool size: the default from the processor count and refused sizes`; `EZD-235b P6 a worker that fails to start or a post that throws fails only that job`; `EZD-235b P7 the pool runs jobs end to end over serveJobs`; `EZD-235b P8 a queued job whose new worker fails to start fails alone and the next queued job retries`; `EZD-235b P9 the default report sends a crash to Sentry at editor.worker_pool`; `EZD-235b J1 probe job sums room areas for at least minMs and counts its passes`; `EZD-235b J2 probe job refuses bad input and accepts the bounds`; `EZD-235b J3 probe job reports a worker scope as worker`; `EZD-235b V1 probe session warms up, then its hook runs the 500 ms job and stop disposes once`; `EZD-235b V2 a failed warm-up sets failed, and a stop before the warm-up sets nothing`; `EZD-235b R1 inject=worker-job mounts the hidden worker probe and nothing else changes`; `EZD-235b W1 done when: a job in a worker leaves no main-thread long task on both rows`; `EZD-235b W2 a main-thread job is counted on both rows`; `EZD-235b W3 a job that rejects or never settles is probe_failed`; `EZD-235b W4 a job that ran too short or not in a worker is refused`; `EZD-235b W5 a probe that never gets ready or failed to start is reported`; `EZD-235b W6 a worker row with no url is no_url, and a run that resolves null is probe_not_worker`; `EZD-235b W7 navigation and HTTP failures are reported`; `EZD-235b W8 a page that navigates away during the job is unexpected_error`; E1 edit `EZD-207c N1 profiles and config`; E2 edit `EZD-207c N2 registry rows`; E3 edit `EZD-017 T7 Budgets table`; G1 the gate's `perf` step on the real build |
